# Jataka Parijata, Chapter 11

...the person born will serve a king and inherit wealth from his father <note>This describes the Sun's influence when positioned in the first house.</note>; if the Moon is there, he will engage in sea-borne trade and be opulent; if Mars, he will be virtuous and have a large <term>male organ</term> <note>original Latin: "membrum virile"</note>; if Mercury, he will have learning, artistic skill, and fame; if Jupiter, he will be honored by his sovereign and devoutly observant of religious vows; if Venus, he will be addicted to women of ill-fame, opulent, and voluptuous.

->If <term>Saturn</term> <note>Manda: the slow-moving planet often associated with old age and restriction.</note> <term>aspects</term> <note>Drishti: the astrological "glance" or influence one planet casts upon another or a specific house.</note> the <term>Ascendant</term> <note>Lagna: the zodiac sign rising on the eastern horizon at the moment of birth, representing the self.</note>, the person will have aged wives, will be unclean, and will be a mischief-maker. If no planet aspects the Ascendant, the astrologer should make his prediction in accordance with the planet ruling the <term>Ascendant Sign</term> <note>Lagna Rasi: the specific constellation of the zodiac that forms the first house.</note>. **45**<-
<note>original Sanskrit: "मन्ददृष्टे विलग्ने तु वृद्धस्त्रीको मली खलः । केनाप्यदृष्टं लग्नं चेद्राशिग्रहवशाद्वदेत् ॥ ४५ ॥"</note>

->When the Ascendant is aspected by its own lord <note>The planet that rules the sign currently rising in the first house.</note>, the person born will be either a king or a favorite of the king, wealthy and well-off. If the Ascendant is aspected by a <term>benefic</term> <note>Saumya: a naturally positive planet like Jupiter, Venus, or a well-placed Mercury.</note> planet, all things will be auspicious. If it is aspected by an <term>evil</term> <note>Papa: a malefic planet like Saturn or Mars.</note> planet, everything will turn out poorly. **46**<-
<note>original Sanskrit: "लग्ने स्वामीक्षिते राजा तत्प्रियो वा धनी सुखी । सौम्येक्षितेऽखिलं सौम्यं पापदृष्टे त्वशोभनम् ॥ ४६ ॥"</note>

->When the Ascendant is aspected by two or more planets, the person born is happy. When it is aspected by all the planets, he will be a king. When there are three benefic planets in the Ascendant, the effect on the person born is that he will make a good king; however, he will be miserable if there is an association with malefic planets. **47**<-
<note>original Sanskrit: "सुखी लग्ने द्वादिदृष्टे सर्वदृष्टे नृपो भवेत् । लग्ने त्रयः शुभा राजा दुःखी पापग्रहान्वयः ॥ ४७ ॥"</note>

If there be three malefic <note>Harmful or challenging planets, such as Saturn, Mars, or Rahu.</note> planets in the <term>Lagna</term> <note>The Ascendant: the sign rising on the eastern horizon at the moment of birth.</note>, he will be unhappy.

> When the lord of the rising sign is extremely powerful, not aspected by malefics, situated in a <term>Kendra</term> <note>Pivot houses: the 1st, 4th, 7th, and 10th houses of a birth chart, considered the pillars of strength.</note> and viewed by benefic planets, he overcomes death and grants a long life accompanied by many virtues and the splendor of a powerful kingship. <note>original Sanskrit: "लग्नाधिपोऽतिबलवानशुभैरदृष्टः केन्द्रस्थितः शुभखगैरवलोक्यमानः । मृत्युं विधूय विदधाति स दीर्घमायुः सार्धं गुणैर्बहुभिरूर्जितराजलक्ष्म्या ॥ ४८ ॥"</note>

*Verse 48.* The lord of the rising sign when possessed of great strength and unaspected by malefic planets, but <term>aspected</term> <note>Drishti: the astrological "glance" or influence one planet casts upon another.</note> by benefic ones and occupying a <term>Kendra</term> position wards off death and secures to the child long life graced with the strong virtues of a vigorous sovereignty.

## ॥ Now, the Results of the Second House ॥
<note>original Sanskrit: "॥ अथ द्वितीयभावफलम् ॥"</note>

> Wealth, eyesight, happiness, knowledge, speech, family, and food—astrologers recognize these as the things arising from the second house, in their proper order. <note>original Sanskrit: "वित्तं नेत्रं सुखं विद्या वाक्कुटुम्बाशनानि च । द्वितीयस्थानजन्यानि क्रमाज्ज्योतिर्विदो विदुः ॥ ४९ ॥"</note>

*Verse 49.* Wealth, visual power, ease, learning, speech, household and food—these, the astrologers recognise as regularly derivable from the 2nd <term>bhava</term> <note>House: a specific sector of the zodiac representing particular areas of life.</note>.

> If the signs of wealth, gain, and the rising sign are occupied by their own lords, the person becomes exceedingly wealthy. Likewise, if the lords of gain and wealth are in the house of gain and are in their own, friendly, or exalted divisions. If the lords of the auspicious divisions and of wealth are in the first house, or if these planets are mutually related, or if the lord of the Ascendant is in the house of wealth or gain, one possesses much wealth. <note>original Sanskrit: "वित्तायोदयराशयः पतिंयुता वित्ताधिको जायते लाभस्थौ धनलाभपौ निजसुहृत्तुङ्गादिगौ चेत्तथा । सद्वर्गामघनाधिपौ तनुगतावन्योन्यमिश्रग्रहौ लग्नेशे धनलाभराशिप्रयुते लग्ने बहुद्रव्यवान् ॥ ५० ॥"</note>

*Verse 50.* A person will have exceeding wealth (1) when the 2nd, the 11th and the 1st <term>bhavas</term> in his horoscope are occupied by their lords; (2) when the

<meta>The text breaks off here as the list of conditions for wealth continues onto the following page.</meta>

<page-num>728</page-num>
<header>728 Jataka Parijata Adhyaya XI.</header>

...lords of the 2nd <note>The house of wealth and family.</note> and the 11th <note>The house of gains and profit.</note> <term>bhavas</term> <note>Astrological houses or signs.</note> are in the 11th house and happen to be in **their own signs** (**Swakshetra**), **friendly signs** (**Mithrakshetra**) or in exaltation <note>The position where a planet is at its maximum strength.</note>; (3) when the lords of the 11th and the 2nd houses, being friendly planets, occupy the <term>Lagna</term> <note>The Ascendant or 1st house.</note> and lastly (4) when the lord of the <term>Lagna</term> is in it in conjunction with the lords of the 2nd and the 11th houses.

->NOTES.<-

This <term>sloka</term> <note>A verse or poetic stanza.</note> is also found in the **Jataka Tattva** <note>A classic 19th-century Sanskrit compendium on predictive astrology.</note>.
The following wealth-producing <term>yogas</term> <note>Specific planetary combinations that produce distinct results.</note> are extracted from
(1) **Uttara Parasara** <note>A supplementary text attributed to the lineage of the sage Parasara, often dealing with advanced predictive techniques.</note>

> When the four houses—the 2nd (Wealth), 9th (Fortune), 11th (Gains), and 10th (Profession)—are strong and occupied by their lords, the person is born with complete wealth, constantly like <term>Kubera</term> <note>The Hindu god of wealth and treasurer of the heavens.</note>. 
> <note>original Sanskrit: "भावैश्चतुर्भिर्बलिभिर्धनभाग्यायकर्म्मभिः । संपूर्णवित्तः सततं कुबेर इव जायते ॥"</note>

> If the lords of the 10th, 11th, and 2nd houses are placed anywhere except in the 6th, 8th, or 12th houses <note>The "Dusthanas" or houses of suffering and loss.</note> from the Ascendant, or if they are mutually well-placed, the mortal born will be wealthy by the hundreds. If these lords are related to each other, they will have wealth in the thousands or tens of thousands; if they are in their own signs or exaltation and in mutual reception, the wealth may reach hundreds of thousands or even millions.
> <note>original Sanskrit: "कर्म्मायस्वशुभेश्वरा रिपुमृतिप्रान्त्यान्विहाय स्थिता... स्वक्षेत्रोच्चपरस्पराश्रयतया लक्षैश्च कोट्यापि वा ॥"</note>

> When the lords of wealth are mutual friends and situated in their signs of exaltation, and are aspected by or joined with benefic planets, there will be abundant wealth. Such a person will possess a truthful and sweet voice and an excellent family. If there is a mixture of influences, the results should be judged accordingly.
> <note>original Sanskrit: "धनेशाधिपती परस्परसुहृत्स्वतुङ्गस्थितौ... वरकुटुम्बमेतद्भवे-"</note>

> If the lords of the 10th, 11th, and 2nd are in the 6th, 8th, or 12th houses from the Ascendant, the person may be impoverished. However, if they are in the 11th, 3rd, or other favorable houses, they will be wealthy. If these lords are situated in the <term>Kendras</term> <note>The 1st, 4th, 7th, and 10th houses.</note> or <term>Trikonas</term> <note>The 1st, 5th, and 9th houses.</note>, wealth is promised. If the number of planets involved is nine, their influence is even greater, though they may be overly attached to women.
> <note>original Sanskrit: "कर्म्मायस्वशुभेश्वरा रिपुमृतिप्रान्तेषु लग्नस्य वा... ते तु प्रसक्ताः स्त्रियः ॥"</note>

> When planets are in friendly or own signs, wealth is in the hundreds; when in exalted signs, it is in the thousands. If the lord of the tens of thousands is also joined there, they become masters of hundreds of thousands. If planets are in mutual exaltation, are friends, or are personally strong, the wealth is counted in millions.
> <note>original Sanskrit: "मितस्वस्थितयो यदा बहुशतैरुच्चस्वभावस्थिता... कोट्यादिभिः संख्यया"</note>

<summary>This page details specific planetary alignments (yogas) that determine a person's level of wealth, ranging from hundreds to millions of units, based on the strength of the 2nd, 9th, 10th, and 11th houses.</summary>
<keywords>wealth yogas, Dhana Bhava, Uttara Parasara, Kubera, planetary strength, 2nd house, 11th house, financial astrology</keywords>

<meta>This page continues the technical exposition of planetary combinations (yogas) that grant wealth, specifically building upon the previous page's citations from the Jataka Tattva and moving into specific rules titled "Jatakarana."</meta>

<page-num>729</page-num>
<header>Verse 51 Chapter Eleven</header>

->Truly, such a man is born on this earth with the most exalted **yogas** <note>planetary combinations</note>.<-
->If the lord of wealth <note>the ruler of the 2nd house</note> is a malefic <note>a planet like Mars or Saturn</note>, then during its **dasha** <note>major planetary period</note>,<-
->during the sub-periods of Rahu, Saturn, or Mars, the lord of wealth [brings results].<-
->If obstructed by a malefic, even if joined with the lord of wealth,<-
->or if a malefic is in the house of wealth, one suffers from constant obstructions and lack of wealth.<-

### (2) **Jatakarana** <note>Literally "the making of the birth," likely referring to a specific set of rules or a treatise concerning the calculation of life events.</note>

If the lord of the sign where the **Lagnaswara** <note>Lord of the Ascendant</note> is placed and the lord of the sign where the ruler of the wealth-sign is placed are both powerful; and if the lord of wealth is in a **Vaiseshikamsa** <note>A high-strength position where a planet occupies its own or exalted sign in several of the sixteen divisional charts</note> or in its exaltation sign, this person is the chief among the wealthy.

When the lord of the wealth-sign is with the Lord of the Ascendant in a **Kendra** <note>A quadrant or angular house: the 1st, 4th, 7th, or 10th</note> and in its highest exaltation **navamsha** <note>The 1/9th divisional sign, used to determine the "inner" strength of a planet</note>, joined with a **Vaiseshikamsa** and aspected by benefic planets, the wise say this **yoga** brings immense wealth and gain.

When the lord of the house of gains <note>the 11th house</note> is with the lord of the house of wealth, and is aspected by the lord of the **navamsha** occupied by the lord of the 10th house <note>the house of profession</note>, and is in a **Vaiseshikamsa** in its highest exaltation, the wise say this **yoga** brings immense wealth and gain.

When the lord of the wealth-sign is with the lord of the 10th house, aspected by the lord of the **navamsha** in which the Lord of the Ascendant is placed, and is located in a quadrant and aspected by benefics, the child born attains immense wealth in their early years.

When a benefic planet is in the Ascendant or the 11th house, or if the moon is in exaltation, a friend's sign, or its own sign, especially in a **Vaiseshikamsa**; and if the lord of wealth or the lord of the **navamsha** is aspecting it, the child attains immense wealth in their early years.

If the Lord of the Ascendant is in the 2nd house, the lord of wealth is in the 11th, and the lord of gains is in the 2nd while possessing full strength; or if they are in their own, exalted, or friendly signs joined with benefics, the person born acquires wealth of his own through many means.

If the Lord of the Ascendant is the strongest among all planets, is situated in a quadrant, and is joined with the **Teacher of the Gods** <note>Jupiter</note>; and if the lord of the wealth-sign is in a **Vaiseshikamsa**, the wise say the person's wealth is earned through his own strength.

If the lord of the sign where the Lord of the Ascendant's **navamsha** ruler is placed is strong, and the lord of wealth is a friend to it and located in a quadrant or a trine <note>the 1st, 5th, or 9th house</note> or in its own sign, the person born acquires wealth of his own through many means.

The first indicator of wealth is the planet occupying the 2nd **bhava** <note>house</note>; the second is the planet aspecting the 2nd house; the third is the lord of wealth itself.
During their **dashas** and sub-periods, one gains wealth according to the nature of the sources indicated by those planets. **(51)**

**Sloka 51.** Note first the planet occupying the 2nd
**bhava**; next, the planet aspecting that bhava; thirdly,
